, respectively, associated to their leading
q_{s}
singular values.
If Assumption
1
–
7
hold and
n_{\min}\gtrsim\sqrt{n_{s}}
for each
s=1,\dots,S
, where
n_{\min}=\min_{s=1,\dots,S}n_{s}
, then

pr\left\{\left|\left|\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\right|\right|>G_{3}(C_{sr,n_{s}}C_{sc,p})^{2}\left(\frac{1}{n_{s}}+\frac{1}{p}\right)\right\}=0\quad(n_{1},\dots n_{S},p\uparrow\infty),

where
G_{3}<\infty
is a finite constant.
Replacing Assumptions
2
and
7
with Assumptions
12
–
18
,
with probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

pr\left\{\left|\left|\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\right|\right|>G_{3}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)\right\}=0\quad(n_{1},\dots n_{S},p\uparrow\infty),

Proof of Proposition
3
.

Recall
\mathbf{Y}_{s}\mathbf{\bar{Q}}=\mathbf{Y}_{s}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{0}\mathbf{V}_{0}^{\top})+\mathbf{Y}_{s}(\mathbf{V}_{0}\mathbf{V}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\bar{P}})
. Next, we apply the same steps as in the proof for Proposition
1
.
In particular,

s_{q_{s}}^{2}(\mathbf{F}_{0s})s_{q_{s}}^{2}((\mathbf{I}_{p}-P_{{\mathbf{\Lambda}}}){\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s})||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}||\leq 4||\mathbf{E}_{s}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{0}\mathbf{V}_{0}^{\top})+\mathbf{Y}_{s}(\mathbf{V}_{0}\mathbf{V}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\bar{P}})||^{2}.

Note that

\displaystyle||\mathbf{E}_{s}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{0}\mathbf{V}_{0}^{\top})||

\displaystyle\leq||\mathbf{E}_{s}||,

\displaystyle||\mathbf{Y}_{s}(\mathbf{V}_{0}\mathbf{V}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\bar{P}})||

\displaystyle\leq||\mathbf{Y}_{s}||||\mathbf{V}_{0}\mathbf{V}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\bar{P}}||,

s_{q_{s}}\left((\mathbf{I}_{p}-P_{{\mathbf{\Lambda}}}){\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}\right)\asymp\sqrt{p}
by Assumption
5
, and
n_{s}=\mathcal{O}(n_{\min}^{2})
, along with
s_{q_{s}}(\mathbf{F}_{0s})\asymp\sqrt{n_{s}}
with probability at least
1-o(1)
by Corollary 5.35 of
Vershynin (2012)
under Assumption
1
or by Assumption
17
. The probabilistic bounds on
||\mathbf{E}_{s}||
and
||\mathbf{Y}_{s}||
in Lemmas
1
and
4
prove the result.
∎

Proposition 4
.

Denote by
\mathbf{U}^{c}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times k_{0}}
and
\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times k_{0}}
the matrices of left singular vectors of
\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c}
and
\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}
, respectively, associated to the leading
k_{0}
singular values.
Then, if Assumptions
1
–
7
hold and
n_{\min}\gtrsim\sqrt{n_{s}}
for each
s=1,\dots,S
, where
n_{\min}=\min_{s=1,\dots,S}n_{s}
, we have

pr\left\{\left|\left|\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\right|\right|>G_{4}(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\left(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{1}{p}\right)\right\}\to 0\quad(n_{1},\dots,n_{S},p\uparrow\infty),

where
G_{4}<\infty
is a finite constant. Moreover, Replacing Assumptions
2
and
7
with Assumptions
12
–
18
,
with probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

pr\left\{\left|\left|\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\right|\right|>G_{4}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)\right\}\to 0\quad(n_{1},\dots,n_{S},p\uparrow\infty).

Proof of Proposition
4
.

We let

\mathbf{N}_{0}^{\perp}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}_{01}^{\perp}&0&&&0\\
0&\mathbf{U}_{02}^{\perp}&0&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&&&&\vdots\\
0&\cdots&&0&\mathbf{U}_{0S}^{\perp}\end{bmatrix}

(30)

and

\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}=\mathbf{N}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{N}_{0}^{\perp\top};

(31)

that is
\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}
is a block diagonal matrix and the
s
-th block is given by
\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}
.
Note that
\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp 2}=\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}
and
\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp\top}=\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}
, since all the blocks are orthogonal projection matrices, making
\mathbf{P}_{0}
an orthogonal projection matrix
Similarly, we define
\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}=\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}
, and
\Delta
to be a block diagonal matrix and the
s
-th block is given
\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}
for
s=1,\dots,S
,

\Delta=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}_{01}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{01}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{1}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{\perp\top}&0&&&0\\
0&\mathbf{U}_{02}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{02}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{2}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{2}^{\perp\top}&0&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&&&&\vdots\\
0&\cdots&&0&\mathbf{U}_{0S}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0S}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{S}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{S}^{\perp\top}\end{bmatrix}.

(32)

Since
\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c}=\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}+\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{E}+\mathbf{\Delta Y}
, we have

\left|\left|\left(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}\right)\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}\right|\right|^{2}\leq 4\left|\left|-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}+\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{E}+\mathbf{\Delta Y}\right|\right|^{2}

by Theorem 2 in
Luo et al. (2021)
. Moreover, we have

||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||\leq||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}||||{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}||\asymp\sqrt{p}
, with probability at least
1-o(1)
, since
||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}||\asymp 1
, with probability at least
1-o(1)
, by Lemma
5
under Assumptions
1
–
7
, or by Assumption
17
, and
||{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}||\asymp\sqrt{p}
by Assumption
4
. The probabilistic bounds in Lemmas
1
and
9
with the fact that
s_{k_{0}}(\mathbf{M}_{0})\asymp\sqrt{n}
with probability at least
1-o(1)
, by Corollary 5.35 of
Vershynin (2012)
under Assumption
2
, or by Assumption
17
, and
s_{k_{0}}({\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0})\asymp\sqrt{p}
by Assumption
4
complete the proof.
∎

A.2
Proofs of the main results

Proof of Theorem
1
.

Let
\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{D}_{0}\mathbf{V}_{0}^{\top}
be the singular value decomposition of
\mathbf{M}_{0}
. Note that
\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}=\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}
where
\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times k_{0}}
is the matrix of left singular vectors of
\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}
. Recall that, under Assumptions
1
–
7
, by Proposition
4
, we have
||\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{1}{p}\big)
, with probability at least
1-o(1)
.
Then, by Davis-Kahan theorem
(
Davis & Kahan 1970
)
we have

\min_{\mathbf{R}:\mathbf{R}^{\top}\mathbf{R}=\mathbf{I}_{k_{0}}}||\mathbf{U}^{c}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{R}||=||\mathbf{U}^{c}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{\hat{R}}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{1}{p}\big)
, with probability at least
1-o(1)
, where
\mathbf{\hat{R}}
is the orthogonal matrix achieving the minimum of the quantity on the left hand side. Recalling that
\mathbf{\hat{M}}=\sqrt{n}\mathbf{U}^{c}
and letting
\mathbf{\tilde{R}}=\mathbf{V}_{0}\mathbf{\hat{R}}
, we have

\displaystyle||\mathbf{\hat{M}}-\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\tilde{R}}||

\displaystyle=||\sqrt{n}\mathbf{U}^{c}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{D}_{0}\mathbf{\hat{R}}||\leq||\sqrt{n}(\mathbf{U}^{c}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{\hat{R}})||+||\sqrt{n}\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{\hat{R}}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{D}_{0}\mathbf{\hat{R}}||

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n}(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{1}{p}\big)+\max_{1\leq l\leq k_{0}}|\sqrt{n}-d_{0l}|

where
d_{0l}
is the
l
-th largest singular value of
\mathbf{D}_{0}
. Moreover, by corollary 5.35 of
Vershynin (2012)
, we have
|d_{0l}-\sqrt{n}|\lesssim\sqrt{k_{0}}
with probability at least
1-o(1)
. We conclude the proof of the first result by noting that
||\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}-\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\tilde{R}}||\leq||\mathbf{\hat{M}}-\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\tilde{R}}||
and
\mathbf{\tilde{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{\tilde{R}}=\mathbf{I}_{k_{0}}
. The proof for the second result proceeds by applying Proposition
3
followed by similar steps. ∎

Proof of Theorem
2
.

First, we show
\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}
is consistent for
{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}
.
We have

\displaystyle\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2})^{2}}\left\{\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c}+\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c\top}\left(\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\right)\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c}\right\}.

Recall from the proof of Proposition
4
that
\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c}=\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}+\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{E}+\mathbf{\Delta Y}
, where
\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}
and
\Delta
are defined in (
31
) and (
32
), respectively, and
\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}=\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}
. Then

\displaystyle\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c}=

\displaystyle n{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}+{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}-n\mathbf{I}_{k_{0}}\right){\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}

\displaystyle+{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{E}+\mathbf{E}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}

\displaystyle+{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{\Delta Y}+\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\Delta}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}

\displaystyle+{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{E}+\mathbf{E}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}

\displaystyle+{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\Delta Y}+\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\Delta}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{E}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{E}+\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\Delta}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\Delta Y}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{E}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\Delta Y}+\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\Delta}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{E}.

We can bound each term, using the fact that, with probability at least
1-o(1)
,

1.

||\mathbf{M}_{0}||\lesssim\sqrt{n}
, by Theorem 4.6.1 of
Vershynin (2018)
,

2.

||\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}-n\mathbf{I}_{k_{0}}||\lesssim(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{k})\sqrt{\log n}
by Lemma E.1 in
Chattopadhyay et al. (2024)
,

3.

||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}||\lesssim\sqrt{k_{0}}+\sqrt{\sum_{s}q_{s}}
by Theorem 4.6.1 of
Vershynin (2018)
, since
\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}\sim MN_{\sum_{s}q_{s},k_{0}}(0,\mathbf{I}_{\sum_{s}q_{s}},\mathbf{I}_{k_{0}})
, where
MN_{d_{1},d_{2}}(\mu,\mathbf{\Sigma}_{r},\mathbf{\Sigma}_{c})
denotes a
d_{1}\times d_{2}
matrix normal distribution with mean
\mu
, within-column covariance
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{c}
and within-row covariance
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{r}
,

4.

||\mathbf{E}||\lesssim C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p}(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p})
, by Lemma
1
,

5.

||\mathbf{\Delta Y}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{\sqrt{p}}{\sqrt{n_{\min}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{\max}}}{\sqrt{p}}\big)
, by Lemma
9
,

and
||{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}||\asymp\sqrt{p}
by Assumption
4
, as well as,
||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}||=||\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}||=||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}||=||\mathbf{N}_{0}^{\perp}||=1
, where
\mathbf{N}_{0}^{\perp}
is defined in (
30
).
Note
\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2})}=\frac{1}{n}+\nu_{n}
where
\nu_{n}\asymp\frac{1}{n^{2}}
and
\nu_{n}||{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}||\asymp\frac{p}{n^{2}}
.
Combining all of the above, we obtain

\displaystyle||\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||\lesssim(\sqrt{\log n}+C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})\frac{p}{\sqrt{n}}+C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p}\sqrt{p}

with probability at least
1-o(1)
.
Finally, since
||{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}||\asymp\sqrt{p}
, with probability at least
1-o(1)
,

\displaystyle\frac{||\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||}{||{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\sqrt{\log n}+C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}+C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}.

By bounding the size of the difference of each sample from the mean, we should also obtain posterior concentration.
A sample
{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}
from
\tilde{\Pi}
is given by

{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}=\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}+\mathbf{\tilde{E}}_{\Lambda},\quad\mathbf{\tilde{E}}_{\Lambda}=[\mathbf{e}_{\lambda_{1}}~\cdots~\mathbf{e}_{\lambda_{p}}]^{\top},\quad\mathbf{e}_{\lambda_{j}}\sim N_{k_{0}}\left(0,\frac{\rho_{\Lambda}^{2}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\mathbf{I}_{k_{0}}\right).

Thus,

\displaystyle\left|\left|{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}-\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}\right|\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\mathbf{\tilde{E}}_{\Lambda}\mathbf{\tilde{E}}_{\Lambda}^{\top}\right|\right|+\left|\left|\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}\mathbf{\tilde{E}}_{\Lambda}^{\top}\right|\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{p}{n}\rho_{\Lambda}^{2}\max_{j}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}+\left(\frac{(np)^{1/2}+n^{1/2}+p^{1/2}}{n^{1/2}}\right)\frac{p^{1/2}}{n^{1/2}}\rho_{\Lambda}^{2}\max_{j}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{p}{n^{1/2}},

with probability at least
1-o(1)
, since
\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}\lesssim 1
with probability at least
1-o(1)
, by Lemma
13
, determining the desired result.

Next, we proceed by establishing consistency for
{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}^{\top}
.
As a first step, we bound
||\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}-\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||
.
Recall that
\mathbf{\hat{M}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}=\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c}
and
\mathbf{\hat{M}_{s}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}=\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c}
, where
\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{s}\times k_{0}}
is the block of
\mathbf{U}^{c}
corresponding to the
s
-th study, that is
\mathbf{U}^{c}=\big[\mathbf{U}_{1}^{c\top}~\dots~\mathbf{U}_{S}^{c\top}\big]^{\top}
.
We have the following decomposition

\displaystyle\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}=\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left\{\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\left(\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}+\mathbf{Q}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{E}+\mathbf{\Delta Y}\right)+\left(\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}\right)\mathbf{\hat{Y}}^{c}\right\}.

Using the fact that, with probability at least
1-o(1)
,

1.

||\mathbf{M}_{0}||\lesssim\sqrt{n}
,
||\mathbf{M}_{0s}||\asymp\sqrt{n_{s}}
, and
||\mathbf{F}_{0s}||\lesssim\sqrt{n_{s}}
by Theorem 4.6.1 of
Vershynin (2018)
,

2.

||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}||\lesssim\sqrt{k_{0}}+\sqrt{\sum_{s}q_{s}}
by Lemma 5,

3.

||\mathbf{E}||\lesssim C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p}(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p})
by Lemma
1
,

4.

||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}||\leq||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{1}{p}\big)
, with probability at least
1-o(1)
by Proposition
4
,

we obtain
||\mathbf{\hat{M}_{s}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}-\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||\leq||\mathbf{\hat{M}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}-\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p})
, with probability at least
1-o(1)
.

Hence, with probability at least
1-o(1)
, for a sample
{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}
from
\tilde{\Pi}
, we have

\displaystyle||\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\tilde{{\mathbf{\Lambda}}}^{\top}-\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||\leq||\mathbf{\hat{M}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}-\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||+||\mathbf{\hat{M}}\left(\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}-{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}\right)^{\top}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p}),

since

||\mathbf{\hat{M}}\left(\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}-{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}\right)^{\top}||\leq||\mathbf{\hat{M}}||||\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}-{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}||\lesssim\sqrt{n}\frac{\sqrt{p}}{\sqrt{n}}\rho_{\Lambda}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}\lesssim\sqrt{p},

and
\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}\lesssim 1
by Lemma
13
with probability
1-o(1)
,
which implies
||\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\tilde{{\mathbf{\Lambda}}}^{\top}-\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p})
, with probability at least
1-o(1)
,
Hence, consider the outer product of the conditional mean of
\mathbf{\Gamma}_{s}
,
\mathbf{\bar{\Gamma}}_{s}=E[{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}\mid\mathbf{Y},\mathbf{\hat{F}}_{s},\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s},\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}},\mathbf{\tilde{\Sigma}},\rho_{\Lambda}]
, given
\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}
,
\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}
and
\mathbf{\tilde{\Sigma}}
, where
(\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}},\mathbf{\tilde{\Sigma}})
is a sample from
\tilde{\Pi}
,

\displaystyle\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top})(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}).

We analyze each term separately.
Since
\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}=\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}+(\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}+\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})=\mathbf{F}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}+\mathbf{E}_{s}+(\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top})+(\mathbf{\hat{M}_{s}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Lambda}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})
.
The first term can be decomposed as follows

\displaystyle\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}

\displaystyle(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})=

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}+\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\left({\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{s}+\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\left\{{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}\right)+\left(\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\left[\left\{\mathbf{E}_{s}+\left(\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}\right)\right\}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\left\{\mathbf{E}_{s}+\left(\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}\right)\right\}\right].

1.

First, we decompose
\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}=\frac{1}{n_{s}}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}+\big(\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}-\frac{1}{n_{s}}\big){\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}
, where
\big(\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}-\frac{1}{n_{s}}\big)\asymp\frac{1}{n_{s}^{2}}
. Then, note

||\big(\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}-\frac{1}{n_{s}}\big){\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}||\lesssim\frac{p}{n_{s}},

and

\frac{1}{n_{s}}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}={\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}+{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\big(\frac{1}{n_{s}}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}-\mathbf{I}_{q_{s}}\big){\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top},

with
||{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\big(\frac{1}{n_{s}}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}-\mathbf{I}_{q_{s}}\big){\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}||\leq||{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}||^{2}||\frac{1}{n_{s}}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}-\mathbf{I}_{q_{s}}||\lesssim\frac{p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}\sqrt{\log{n_{s}}}
,
with probability at least
1-o(1)
, since

||\frac{1}{n_{s}}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}-\mathbf{I}_{q_{s}}||\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log{n_{s}}}}{n_{s}}
with probability at least
1-o(1)
by Corollary E.1 of
Chattopadhyay et al. (2024)
.

2.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}||{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{s}+\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n_{s}}||{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}||||\mathbf{F}_{0s}||||\mathbf{E}_{s}||\lesssim\frac{1}{n_{s}}\sqrt{n_{s}p}C_{sr,n_{s}}C_{sc,p}(\sqrt{n_{s}}+\sqrt{p})

\displaystyle=C_{sr,n_{s}}C_{sc,p}(\sqrt{p}+\frac{p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}).

3.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}

\displaystyle||\left\{\mathbf{E}_{s}+\left(\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}\right)\right\}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\left\{\mathbf{E}_{s}+\left(\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}\right)\right\}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n_{s}}(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{4}\left(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p}\right)^{2}\asymp(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{4}\big(\frac{n}{n_{s}}+\frac{p}{n_{s}}+\frac{\sqrt{np}}{n_{s}}\big),

since, with probability at least
1-o(1)
,
||\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p})
and
||\mathbf{E}_{s}||\lesssim C_{sr,n_{s}}C_{sc,p}(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p})
by Lemma
1
.
For the second term, since
||\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}||\leq||\mathbf{F}_{s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}^{\top}||+||\mathbf{E}_{s}||+||\mathbf{\hat{M}}\tilde{{\mathbf{\Lambda}}}^{\top}-\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||\lesssim\sqrt{n_{s}p}+(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p})\asymp\sqrt{n_{s}p}

with probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}

\displaystyle||(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top})(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n_{s}}||\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}||^{2}||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n_{s}}n_{s}p(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\left(\frac{1}{n_{s}}+\frac{1}{p}\right)

\displaystyle\asymp(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(1+\frac{p}{n_{s}}\big).

Combining all of the above, with probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle||\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}||\lesssim(C_{sr,n_{s}}C_{sc,p})\big(\sqrt{p}+\frac{p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}\big)+\frac{\sqrt{\log n_{s}}p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}.

The assumption
||{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}||\asymp\sqrt{p}
, implies

\displaystyle\frac{||\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}||}{||{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{0s}^{\top}||}\lesssim(\sqrt{\log n_{s}}+C_{sr,n_{s}}C_{sc,p})\frac{p}{\sqrt{n}}+C_{sr,n_{s}}C_{sc,p}\sqrt{p}

with probability at least
1-o(1)
.
Given the corresponding sample for
{\mathbf{\Lambda}}
, a sample for
{\mathbf{\tilde{\Gamma}}}_{s}
from
\tilde{\Pi}
is given by

{\mathbf{\tilde{\Gamma}}}_{s}=\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}+\mathbf{\mathbf{\tilde{E}}}_{\Gamma_{s}},\quad\mathbf{\mathbf{\tilde{E}}}_{\Gamma_{s}}=[\mathbf{e}_{\gamma_{s1}}~\cdots~\mathbf{e}_{\gamma_{sp}}]^{\top},\quad\mathbf{e}_{\gamma_{sj}}\sim N_{q_{s}}\left(0,\frac{\rho_{\Gamma_{s}}^{2}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{I}_{q_{s}}\right),

where
{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}=\frac{\sqrt{n_{s}}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}(\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top})^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}
and

\displaystyle||\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}||

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}||\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}(||\mathbf{Y}_{s}-\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}||+||\mathbf{M}_{0s}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Lambda}}}^{\top}||)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}\sqrt{n_{s}p}\asymp\sqrt{p}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\left|\left|{\mathbf{\tilde{\Gamma}}}_{s}{\mathbf{\tilde{\Gamma}}}_{s}^{\top}-\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}^{\top}\right|\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\mathbf{\mathbf{\tilde{E}}}_{\Gamma_{s}}\mathbf{\mathbf{\tilde{E}}}_{\Gamma_{s}}^{\top}\right|\right|+\left|\left|\bar{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{s}\mathbf{\mathbf{\tilde{E}}}_{\Gamma_{s}}^{\top}\right|\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{p}{n_{s}}\rho_{\Gamma_{s}}^{2}\max_{j}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}+\frac{p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}\rho_{\Gamma_{s}}^{2}\max_{j}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{p}{n_{s}^{1/2}},

since
\max_{j}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}\asymp 1
, with
\tilde{\Pi}
probability at least
1-o(1)
, by Lemma
13
, determining the desired result.
∎

Proof of Theorem
3
.

Follows as a special case of Theorem
5

∎

Theorem 5
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
–
10
hold, the residual error variance matrices
\{\mathbf{\Sigma}_{0sc}\}_{s}
are equal across studies,
n_{s}=\mathcal{O}(n_{\min}^{2})
, where
n_{\min}=\min_{s=1,\dots,S}n_{s}
, for all
s=1,\dots,S
,
(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\sqrt{n}/p=o(1)
and
\log^{2}p/\sqrt{n_{\min}}
. For
1\leq j\leq j^{\prime}\leq p
, let

\mathcal{S}_{0jj^{\prime}}^{2}=\begin{cases}\sigma_{0j}^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||^{2}+\sigma_{0j^{\prime}}^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}+\sigma_{0jj^{\prime}}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}+||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||^{2}+(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}})^{2}\quad&\text{if }j\neq j^{\prime},\\
2||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{4}+4\sigma_{0j}^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}\quad&\text{otherwise,}\end{cases}

(33)

and

\mathcal{S}_{0sjj^{\prime}}^{2}=\begin{cases}\begin{aligned} &\sigma_{0j}^{2}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||^{2}+\sigma_{0j^{\prime}}^{2}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{2}+\sigma_{0jj^{\prime}}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}+||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{2}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||^{2}\quad\\
&+(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}})^{2}+||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}+||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}+2\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}\end{aligned}\quad&\text{if }j\neq j^{\prime},\\
2||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{4}+4||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}+4\sigma_{0j}^{2}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{2}&\text{otherwise,}\end{cases}

(34)

where
\sigma_{0jj^{\prime}}
denotes the covariance between the
j
-th and
j^{\prime}
-th variables.
Then, as
n_{1},\dots,n_{s},p\to\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{n}}{S_{0,jj^{\prime}}}\left(\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}-\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}\right)\Longrightarrow N(0,1),

(35)

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{n_{s}}}{S_{0,sjj^{\prime}}}\left(\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj}}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj^{\prime}}}-\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}\right)\Longrightarrow N(0,1),\quad(s=1,\dots,S).

Proof of Theorem
5
.

We start by proving the result for
\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}
. Consider

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}\left\{\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\left(\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\right)\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}\right\}.

Let
\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}+\nu_{n}
, where
\nu_{n}\asymp 1/n^{3/2}
,

\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}=\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}+\mathbf{e}^{(j)}+\mathbf{r}^{(j)}
,
\mathbf{r}^{(j)}=\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}(\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}+\mathbf{e}^{(j)})
, and
\Delta
and
\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}
are defined in (
32
) and (
31
), respectively. First, we decompose
\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}
as

\displaystyle\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}+\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j)}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{e}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j)}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{r}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{e}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}.

Note that
\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{i})(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{i})
, with
E[(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{i})(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{i})]=\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}
, and

\mathbb{V}[(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{i})(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{i})]=\xi_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}
, where

\xi_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}=\begin{cases}(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}})^{2}+||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||^{2},\quad&\text{if }j\neq j^{\prime},\\
2||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{4}&\text{otherwise.}\end{cases}

In addition, the
(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{i})(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{i})
’s are independent of each other.
Therefore, an application of the central limit theorem gives

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}\Longrightarrow N(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}},\xi_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}).

Next, for
j\neq j^{\prime}
, let

\displaystyle l_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{0})

\displaystyle=\sigma_{0j}^{2}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\frac{\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}}{n}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}+\sigma_{0j^{\prime}}^{2}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\frac{\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}}{n}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}+\sigma_{0jj^{\prime}}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\frac{\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}}{n}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}},

\displaystyle l_{0jj^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{0j}^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||^{2}+\sigma_{0j^{\prime}}^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}+\sigma_{0jj^{\prime}}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}},

and note

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}\right)\mid\mathbf{M}_{0}

\displaystyle\sim N(0,l_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{0}))\overset{d}{=}l_{jj^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{0})z_{jj^{\prime}},

\displaystyle z_{jj^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\sim N(0,1),\quad z_{jj^{\prime}}\perp\mathbf{M}_{0},

where
\overset{d}{=}
denotes equality in distribution. Hence,

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}\right)=l_{0jj^{\prime}}z_{jj^{\prime}}+\left(l_{jj^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{0})-l_{0jj^{\prime}}\right)z_{jj^{\prime}}.

Since
||\mathbf{M}_{0}||\asymp\sqrt{n}
, we have
|l_{jj^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{0})+l_{0jj^{\prime}}|\asymp 1
with probability at least
1-o(1)
, and
l_{jj^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{0})+l_{0jj^{\prime}}>0
.

\displaystyle l_{0jj^{\prime}}z_{jj^{\prime}}+\left(l_{jj^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{0})-l_{0jj^{\prime}}\right)z_{jj^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=l_{0jj^{\prime}}z_{jj^{\prime}}+\frac{\left(l_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{0})-l_{0jj^{\prime}}^{2}\right)}{l_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{0})+l_{0jj^{\prime}}^{2}}z_{jj^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow N(0,l_{0jj^{\prime}}^{2}),

since
\left|\left|\frac{\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}}{n}-\mathbf{I}_{k_{0}}\right|\right|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log n}}{\sqrt{n}}
with probability
1-o(1)
by Lemma E.1 of
Chattopadhyay et al. (2024)
, making
\frac{\left(l_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{0})-l_{0jj^{\prime}}^{2}\right)}{l_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}(\mathbf{M}_{0})+l_{0jj^{\prime}}^{2}}z_{jj^{\prime}}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log n}}{\sqrt{n}}
with probability at least
1-o(1)
.
For
j=j^{\prime}
, we can show with similar steps

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}2\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}\Longrightarrow N(0,4\sigma_{0j}^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}).

Combining the results above and using the fact that elements of
\mathbf{M}_{0}
are uncorrelated with
\mathbf{e}^{(j)}
and
\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}
, we have

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}+\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}\right)\Longrightarrow N(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}},\xi_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}+l_{0jj^{\prime}}^{2}).

In the rest of the proof, we show the remaining terms can be suitably bounded by sequences decreasing to 0.
In the following, we use the fact that, with probability at least
1-o(1)
,

1.

\max_{j=1,\dots,p}||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||\lesssim\log p
by Lemma
6
,

2.

\max_{j=1,\dots,p}||\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||\lesssim\log p
by Lemma
5
,

3.

||\mathbf{M}_{0}||\asymp\sqrt{n}
by Theorem 4.6.1 of
Vershynin (2018)
,

4.

||\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}||\asymp 1
by Lemma
5
,

5.

\max_{j=1,\dots,p}||\mathbf{\Delta y}^{(j)}||\asymp C_{\sigma}C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{\min}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{\max}}}{p}\big)
by Lemma
9
,

6.

||\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}||\asymp C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{\min}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{\max}}}{p})+\log p
with probability at least
1-o(1)
by Lemma
10
,

7.

\max_{j=1,\dots,p}||\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||\lesssim\sqrt{\log p}
by Corollary 5.35 of
Vershynin (2012)
,

8.

||\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{1}{p}\big)
by Proposition
4
.

along with
\max_{j,\dots,p}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}|\lesssim||{\mathbf{\Lambda}}||_{\infty}\sqrt{k_{0}}\asymp 1
by Assumption
4
.

1.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}\left|\mathbf{e}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}\right|\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}||\lesssim\frac{\log p}{\sqrt{n}}.

2.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}\right|=\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}\left|\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left[||\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}||\left\{||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||||\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}||+||\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}||+||\mathbf{M}_{0}||||\mathbf{\Delta}y^{c(j^{\prime})}||\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log p}{\sqrt{n}}+C_{\sigma}C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{\min}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{\max}}}{p}\big).

Similarly,
\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}\big|\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j)}\big|\lesssim\frac{\log p}{\sqrt{n}}+C_{\sigma}C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{\min}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{\max}}}{p}\big)
with probability at least
1-o(1)
.

3.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}\left|\mathbf{e}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}||\mathbf{e}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}||||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}||\mathbf{e}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}||||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}||||\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}C_{\sigma}C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{\min}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{\max}}}{p}\big)\log p+\frac{\log p^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}.

Similarly,
\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}\left|\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j)}\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}C_{\sigma}C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{\min}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{\max}}}{p}\big)\log p+\frac{\log p^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}
with probability at least
1-o(1)
.

4.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}\left|\mathbf{r}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}||\mathbf{r}^{(j)\top}||||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||||\mathbf{r}^{(j^{\prime})}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\big\{C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{\min}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{\max}}}{p})+\log p\big\}.

5.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\nu_{n}\big(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}+

\displaystyle\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{n}\big(||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||||\mathbf{M}_{0}||^{2}+||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||||\mathbf{M}_{0}||||\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}||

\displaystyle+||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||||\mathbf{M}_{0}||||\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}(n+\log p)\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\log p}{n}.

6.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}\big|\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\big(\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\big)\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{n}\frac{n}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-1})^{2}}||\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}||||\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}||||\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}n(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{1}{p}\big)\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{n}}{p}\big).

To complete the proof, we apply Lemma F.2 from
Chattopadhyay et al. (2024)
.

Next, we move to
\mathbf{\mu}_{\Gamma_{s}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\Gamma_{s}}^{\top}
. Recall the posterior mean for
\mathbf{\gamma}_{sj}

\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj}}=\frac{1}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{\hat{F}_{s}}^{\top}(\mathbf{y}_{sj}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}).

Consider the singular value decomposition of
(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}^{\top}=\mathbf{U}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}^{\prime\top}
. Following similar steps to those in the proof of Proposition
4
, we can show that
||\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{\prime\top}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{1}{p}\big)
with probability at least
1-o(1)
. Moreover, define
\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{s}\times k_{0}}
to be the block of
\mathbf{U}^{\prime}\in R^{n\times k_{0}}
corresponding to the
s
-th study, that is
\mathbf{U}^{\prime}=\big[\mathbf{U}_{1}^{{}^{\prime}\top}~\cdots~\mathbf{U}_{S}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\big]
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}=\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}=\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left\{\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}+(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\right\}.

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})(\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}+\mathbf{e}^{(j)})+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{I}_{n_{s}}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top})\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}+\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}-\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}=\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\big[

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}-\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\big]+\frac{\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}_{sj}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}=\mathbf{y}_{sj}-\mathbf{M}_{0s}

\displaystyle\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}+(\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}-\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}})

\displaystyle\quad=-\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\big[

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}-\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\big]+\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}+\frac{\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j},

\displaystyle\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{\hat{F}_{s}}^{\top}\big[\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}+\frac{1}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left(n\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}-\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}\mathbf{I}_{n_{s}}\right)\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}+(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{n_{s}}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}\big[\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}+\frac{1}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left(n\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}-\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}\mathbf{I}_{n_{s}}\right)\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}+(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\big]

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n_{s}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj}}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})}+\frac{1}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left(n\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}-\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}\mathbf{I}_{n_{s}}\right)\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j^{\prime})}\right\}+(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}\right]

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\left[\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}+\frac{1}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left(n\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}-\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}\mathbf{I}_{n_{s}}\right)\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}+(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})}+\frac{1}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left(n\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}-\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}\mathbf{I}_{n_{s}}\right)\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j^{\prime})}\right\}+(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}\right]

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\right)

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}+\frac{1}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left(n\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}-\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}\mathbf{I}_{n_{s}}\right)\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}\right]

First, note the following

\displaystyle\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}=\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}=\sum_{i=1}^{n_{s}}(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}=\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}=\sum_{i=1}^{n_{s}}(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})e_{sij},

\displaystyle\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}=\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}=\sum_{i=1}^{n_{s}}(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{0si}).

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle E[(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj},

\displaystyle V[(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})]

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}})^{2}+||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{2}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||^{2},\quad&\text{if }j\neq j^{\prime},\\
2||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{4}&\text{otherwise,}\end{cases}

\displaystyle E[(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})e_{sij}]

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle V[(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})e_{sij}]

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\sigma_{0j}^{2}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||^{2}+\sigma_{0j^{\prime}}^{2}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{2}+\sigma_{0jj^{\prime}}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}},\quad&\text{if }j\neq j^{\prime},\\
4\sigma_{0j}^{2}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{2}&\text{otherwise,}\\
\end{cases}

\displaystyle E[(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{0si})]

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle V[(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{0si})]

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}+||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}+2\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}},\quad&\text{if }j\neq j^{\prime},\\
4||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2}&\text{otherwise,}\\
\end{cases}

and

\displaystyle cov((\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si}),(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})e_{sij})

\displaystyle=cov((\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si}),(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{0si}))

\displaystyle=cov((\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})e_{sij},(\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{\phi}_{0si})(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\eta}_{0si}))=0,

for all
i=1,\dots,n_{s}
and
j,j^{\prime}=1,\dots,p
.
Next, we bound the remaining terms using the fact that, with probability at least
1-o(1)
,

1.

||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}||\leq||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}||\lesssim 1
by Lemma
5
,

2.

\max_{j=1,\dots,p}||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}||\lesssim\max_{j=1,\dots,p}||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||\lesssim\log p
by Lemma
5
,

3.

\max_{j=1,\dots p}||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||\lesssim\log
by Lemma
6
,

4.

||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}||\lesssim 1
by Lemma
7
,

along with
\max_{j,\dots,p}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}|\lesssim||{\mathbf{\Lambda}}||_{\infty}\sqrt{k_{0}}\asymp 1
by Assumption
4
.
With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}n^{2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2})^{2}}

\displaystyle\big|\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}\big|

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}n^{2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2})^{2}}\big|\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}\big|

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}\big|\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}\big|

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}||^{2}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}},

\displaystyle\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}n}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2})}

\displaystyle\big|\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}\big|

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}n}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2})}\big|\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}\big|

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}\big|\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}\big|

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}},

similarly,
\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\frac{n^{2}}{(n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2})^{2}}\big|\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})}\big|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}
,
and

\displaystyle\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\big|\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}\big|

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}\big|\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}\big|

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log^{2}p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}.

Combining all of the above, we get

\displaystyle\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}+\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})}+\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}\right)\Longrightarrow N(\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}^{\top}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}},S_{0,sjj^{\prime}}^{2})

Finally, we show that all the remaining terms decrease to
0
at the appropriate rate, using the fact that, with probability at least
1-o(1)
,

1.

||\mathbf{M}_{0}||\lesssim\sqrt{n}
,
||\mathbf{M}_{0s}||\asymp\sqrt{n_{s}}
, and
||\mathbf{F}_{0s}||\lesssim\sqrt{n_{s}}
by Theorem 4.6.1 of
Vershynin (2018)
,

2.

||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}||\leq||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{M}_{0}||\lesssim 1
, by Lemma
5
,

3.

\max_{j=1,\dots,p}||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}||=\max_{j=1,\dots,p}||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}||\lesssim\max_{j=1,\dots,p}||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||\lesssim\log p
by Lemma
5
,

4.

\max_{j=1,\dots p}||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||\lesssim\log
by Lemma
6
,

5.

||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}||\lesssim 1
by Lemma
7
.

6.

\max_{j=1,\dots p}||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||\lesssim\log
by Lemma
6
,

7.

\max_{j=1,\dots,p}||\mathbf{\Delta y}^{(j)}||\asymp C_{\sigma}C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{\min}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{\max}}}{p}\big)
by Lemma
9
,

8.

||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}||\lesssim(C_{sr,n_{s}}C_{sc,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{n}
)
by Proposition
3
.

along with
\max_{j,\dots,p}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}|\lesssim||{\mathbf{\Lambda}}||_{\infty}\sqrt{k_{0}}\asymp 1
and
\max_{j,\dots,p}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}|\lesssim||{\mathbf{\Gamma}_{s}}||_{\infty}\sqrt{q_{s}}\asymp 1
by Assumptions
4
and
5
, respectively.

1.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{n_{s}}\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\left[-\frac{\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}+\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sqrt{n_{s}}\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\left[-\frac{\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}++\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\quad+\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n_{s}^{3/2}}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}||+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n_{s}^{3/2}}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||||\mathbf{F}_{0s}||||\mathbf{M}_{0s}||||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||||\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}||||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||||\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||||\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}||+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||||\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||||\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}^{\top}||||\mathbf{F}_{0s}||||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}||||\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}n_{s}(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n_{s}}+\frac{1}{p}\big)\asymp(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{s}}}{p}\big).

Similarly, with probability at least
1-o(1)
,

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{n_{s}}\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{0s}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\left[-\frac{\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}+\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j^{\prime})}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad+\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}\right]\right|\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{s}}}{p}\big).

2.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{n_{s}}\frac{n_{s}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}\right|

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log^{2}p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}.

3.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\left[-\frac{\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}+\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n_{s}^{3/2}}||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n_{s}^{3/2}}||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0s}||||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}||||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}(\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}+\mathbf{e}^{(j)})||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||||\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}||

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}||||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}||||\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}||

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log^{2}p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}.

Similarly, with probability at least
1-o(1)
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\left[-\frac{\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}+\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j^{\prime})}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}\right]\right|\lesssim\frac{\log^{2}p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}.

.

4.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\bigg|\big[-\frac{\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}+\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j^{\prime})}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}\big]^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}

\displaystyle\big[-\frac{\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}\mathbf{M}_{0s}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}+\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top})\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}\big]\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}\big\{\frac{1}{n_{s}}||\mathbf{M}_{0s}||||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||+||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}||||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}||+||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||(||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}||+||\Delta y^{(j^{\prime})}||)

\displaystyle+||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}||||\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j^{\prime})}||\big\}||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}||\big\{\frac{1}{n_{s}}||\mathbf{M}_{0s}||||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||

\displaystyle+||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}||||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}||+||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||(||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}||+||\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}||)

\displaystyle+||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}||||\mathbf{\hat{y}}^{c(j)}||\big\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log^{2}p}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}+(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{s}}}{p}\big).

5.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n_{s}^{3/2}}{(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})^{2}}\bigg|\big[\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})}+\frac{1}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left(n\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}-\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}I\right)\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j^{\prime})}\right\}\big]^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\right)

\displaystyle\Big[\mathbf{F}_{0s}\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}+\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}+\frac{1}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left(n\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}-\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}I\right)\mathbf{M}_{0}\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{e}^{(j)}+\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}\right\}\Big]\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}\big(||\mathbf{F}_{0s}||||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj^{\prime}}||+||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j^{\prime})}||+||\mathbf{U}_{0s}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}||+\frac{1}{n}||\mathbf{M}_{0}||||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j^{\prime}}||

\displaystyle+||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}||||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}||+||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{e}^{(j^{\prime})}||+||\Delta y^{(j^{\prime})}||\big)

\displaystyle||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}||

\displaystyle\big(||\mathbf{F}_{0s}||||\mathbf{\gamma}_{0sj}||+||\mathbf{e}_{s}^{(j)}||+||\mathbf{U}_{0s}||||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{M}_{0}||+\frac{1}{n}||\mathbf{M}_{0}||||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||

\displaystyle+||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}||||\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||+||\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{c}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{c\top}||||\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp}\mathbf{e}^{(j)}||+||\mathbf{\Delta}y^{(j)}||\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim(C_{sr,n_{s}}C_{sc,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{s}}}+\frac{\sqrt{n_{s}}}{p}\big).

To complete the proof, we apply Lemma F.2 from
Chattopadhyay et al. (2024)
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
4
.

First, we show the result for
\mathbf{\tilde{\lambda}}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{\tilde{\lambda}}_{j^{\prime}}
. A sample
\mathbf{\tilde{\lambda}}_{j}
from
\tilde{\Pi}
for
\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}
is given by

\mathbf{\tilde{\lambda}}_{j}=\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}+\frac{\rho_{\Lambda}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}}{\sqrt{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}},\quad\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}}\sim N_{k_{0}}\left(0,\mathbf{I}_{k_{0}}\right),\quad(j=1,\dots,p).

Thus, for
j\neq j^{\prime}
,

\mathbf{\tilde{\lambda}}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{\tilde{\lambda}}_{j^{\prime}}=\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}+\frac{\rho_{\Lambda}\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}^{\top}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}}+\frac{\rho_{\Lambda}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}}{\sqrt{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}^{\top}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}}+\rho_{\Lambda}^{2}\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{j}\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}}}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}}^{\top}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}}.

We examine each term separately.
Let

l^{2}(\rho_{\Lambda},\tilde{\sigma}_{j},\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}},\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}},\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j^{\prime}}})=\frac{\rho_{\Lambda}^{2}n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\left(\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}}^{2}||\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}||^{2}+\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}||\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}||^{2}\right),

and note

\mathbf{\tilde{\lambda}}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{\tilde{\lambda}}_{j^{\prime}}=\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}+l_{0,jj^{\prime}}(\rho_{\Lambda})z_{jj^{\prime}}+\{l(\rho_{\Lambda},\tilde{\sigma}_{j},\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}},\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}},\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j^{\prime}}})-l_{0,jj^{\prime}}(\rho_{\Lambda})\}z_{jj^{\prime}}+\rho_{\Lambda}^{2}\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{j}\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}}}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}}^{\top}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}},

where
z_{jj^{\prime}}\sim N(0,1)
.

1.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\sqrt{n}\rho_{\Lambda}^{2}\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{j}\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}}}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}|\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}}^{\top}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},

since
\tilde{\sigma}_{u}=\mathcal{O}_{pr}(1)
for
u=j,j^{\prime}
by Lemma
13
and
||\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}}||\asymp||\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}}||\asymp 1
with
\tilde{\Pi}
probability at least
1-o(1)
by Corollary 5.35 of
Vershynin (2012)
.

2.

Since
||\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{u}}||^{2}\to||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0u}||
and
\tilde{\sigma}_{u}^{2}\to\sigma_{0u}^{2}
in probability for
u=j,j^{\prime}
by Lemma
11
, and
\frac{n}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}\asymp 1
as
n\to\infty
, we have
l(\rho_{\Lambda},\tilde{\sigma}_{j},\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}},\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}},\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j^{\prime}}})-l_{0,jj^{\prime}}(\rho_{\Lambda})\to 0
in probability for any finite
\rho_{\Lambda}
.

For
j=j^{\prime}
, we have

||\mathbf{\tilde{\lambda}}_{j}||^{2}=||\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}||^{2}+2\frac{\rho_{\Lambda}\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}^{\top}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}}+\frac{\rho_{\Lambda}^{2}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}||\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}}||^{2}.

Similarly as above, we have
2\frac{\rho_{\Lambda}\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}}\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}}\mathbf{\mu}_{\lambda_{j}}^{\top}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}}\Longrightarrow N(0,4\rho_{\Lambda}^{2}\sigma_{0j}^{2}||\mathbf{\lambda}_{0j}||^{2})
and
\rho_{\Lambda}^{2}\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}\sqrt{n}}{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}||\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}}||^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}
with probability at least
1-o(1)
for all finite
\rho_{\Lambda}
.

Consider the following representation of a sample for
\mathbf{\gamma}_{sj}
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\tilde{\gamma}}_{sj}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj}}+\frac{\rho_{\Lambda}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}}{\sqrt{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})}\mathbf{\hat{F}_{s}}^{\top}\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}}+\frac{\rho_{\Gamma_{s}}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}}{\sqrt{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\gamma}_{sj}},

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j}}

\displaystyle\sim N_{k_{0}}(0,\mathbf{I}_{k_{0}}),\quad\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\gamma}_{sj}}\sim N_{q_{s}}(0,\mathbf{I}_{q_{s}}).

Following the same steps as in the Proof for Theorem
4
and using Lemma
12
, we can show

\sqrt{n}\bigg(\frac{\rho_{\Gamma_{s}}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}}{\sqrt{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\gamma}_{sj}}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj^{\prime}}}+\frac{\rho_{\Gamma_{s}}\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2}}}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\gamma}_{sj^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj}}\bigg)\Longrightarrow N(0,l_{0,sjj^{\prime}}^{2}(\rho_{\Gamma_{s}})).

We complete the proof by showing the remaining terms in
\sqrt{n}\mathbf{\tilde{\gamma}}_{sj}^{\top}\mathbf{\tilde{\gamma}}_{sj^{\prime}}
converge to
0
at the appropriate rate.

1.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\big|\frac{\sqrt{n_{s}}\rho_{\Lambda}\tilde{\sigma}_{j}}{\sqrt{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})}\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj}}^{\top}\mathbf{\hat{F}_{s}}^{\top}\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}}\big|\asymp\big|\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}}\big|\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n_{s}}+\frac{1}{p}\big),

since
||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n_{s}}+\frac{1}{p}\big)
with probability at least
1-o(1)
as we show in the following. In particular, from the proof of Theorem
3
, we have
||\mathbf{U}^{c}\mathbf{U}^{c\top}-\mathbf{U}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{{}^{\prime}\top}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{1}{p}\big)
with probability at least
1-o(1)
, where
\mathbf{U}^{\prime}
is the matrix of left singular vectors of
(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{P}_{0}^{\perp})\mathbf{M}_{0}{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{0}^{\top}
. Then, by Davis-Kahan theorem
(
Davis & Kahan 1970
)
we have

\min_{\mathbf{R}:\mathbf{R}^{\top}\mathbf{R}=\mathbf{I}_{k_{0}}}||\mathbf{U}^{c}-\mathbf{U}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}||=||\mathbf{U}^{c}-\mathbf{U}^{\prime}\mathbf{\hat{R}}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{1}{p}\big)
, with probability at least
1-o(1)
, where
\mathbf{\hat{R}}
is the orthogonal matrix achieving the minimum of the quantity on the left hand side. Similarly, we have
\min_{\mathbf{R}_{s}:\mathbf{R}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{s}=\mathbf{I}_{q_{s}}}||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}R||=||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{\hat{R}}_{s}||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n_{s}}+\frac{1}{p}\big)
, with probability at least
1-o(1)
. Therefore, with probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

||\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}||=||(\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{\hat{R}}_{s}+\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp}-\mathbf{U}_{0s}^{\perp}\mathbf{\hat{R}}_{s})^{\top}(\mathbf{U}^{\prime}\mathbf{\hat{R}}+\mathbf{U}^{c}-\mathbf{U}^{\prime}\mathbf{\hat{R}})||\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n_{s}}+\frac{1}{p}\big)
, since
\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}^{\prime}=0
. Analogously, with probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\sqrt{n_{s}}\rho_{\Lambda}\tilde{\sigma}_{j^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{n+\tau_{\Lambda}^{-2}}(n_{s}+\tau_{\Gamma_{s}}^{-2})}\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj}}^{\top}\mathbf{\hat{F}_{s}}^{\top}\mathbf{\hat{M}}_{s}\mathbf{\eta}_{j}\right|\asymp\big|\mathbf{\mu}_{\gamma_{sj}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{\perp\top}\mathbf{U}_{s}^{c}\varepsilon_{\mathbf{\lambda}_{j^{\prime}}}\big|\lesssim(C_{r,\{n_{s}\}_{s}^{S}}C_{c,p})^{2}\big(\frac{1}{n_{s}}+\frac{1}{p}\big).

2.

With probability at least
1-o(1)
, we have
